### Early-game build [built]
Run the agents **one at a time** (never in parallel). They add to the same shared files and their Gradle builds would collide. Run `mod-tester` after each step.
1. **`portal-builder`**:
   - Add the shared "Palette" section to `DrossColors`, and move the portal's colors onto `DrossColors` (blue).
   - The Rift Key item, and key activation instead of the netherite ingot (used up; pops back if the frame is already lit; never inside the Dross).
   - Block nether-portal lighting in the Dross.
   - Blue portal texture recolor, particles, overlay and title.
   - The original frame texture, plus the frame's ambient particles and sound.
   - `DrossHub` (center constant, template or placeholder, lit exit portal, built once). Arrival at the hub, exit to the castle, and removing the auto-built return portal and the saved return-portal lookup.
   - The Rift Key and frame in the creative tab.
   - Until Quest exists, keep the current advancement grant in `DrossArrival`. Step 2's agent will need the hook points listed in your report.
2. **`quest-builder`**:
   - The progress `SavedData` and its public API (including the automatic "Proven Worthy" / "Keymaster" grants).
   - The grant helper.
   - The Dross tab: root plus all advancement JSON. That means adding `weathered_letter`, `proven_worthy` and `keymaster`, and moving `entered_the_dross` and `rise` into the tab.
   - The Weathered Letter, its loot modifier and `registry/ModLootModifiers`.
   - The Dross Guide Book item and the first-arrival flag.
   - The `/dross quest` logic.
   - Report the API for Portal (Guide Book and arrival grant), Enchantments ("Rise!") and Villager.
3. **`villager-builder`**:
   - Dialogue, tribute and key-material hand-ins through Quest's API, the compass gift and free replacement, and the Rift Key gift.
   - The new shop (quest-gated, replacing old offers on load), and removing all test trades.
   - Removing `TestingJoinMessage`.
   - Blue glow and spawn egg via `DrossColors`.
   - Aiming the compass at `PortalSite.getOpeningCenter` once World site adds it (until then keep the current target and report "Needs from world site").
4. **`world-builder`**:
   - The castle (template or placeholder shrine) on flattened ground, once per world, finding the frame and storing its corner and axis.
   - Making `PortalSite` axis-aware (`getFrameAxis`, `getOpeningCenter`).
   - The leak effects.
   - An axis-aware `/dross goto cathedral`.
   - Hooking up `/dross goto hub` and `/dross quest`.
5. **`dimension-builder`**: no natural hostile spawns within 48 blocks of `DrossHub.CENTER`.
6. **`enchant-builder`**: blue wisps, puffs and Deathforged X glow via `DrossColors`. Grant "Rise!" through Quest's helper.
7. **Follow-ups**: any "Needs from <area>" items from the reports above (for example Portal switching the arrival grant and Guide Book to Quest's API, or Villager switching the compass to `getOpeningCenter`). Then a final `mod-tester` run (build plus a `runClient` load check).

### Technical notes for the early-game build
- **Ingot activation:** `portal/DrossPortalActivation.java` checks `Items.NETHERITE_INGOT` (around L45 and L60). The shrink-by-one step stays; add the dimension check and the "already lit" pop-back.
- **Teleport:** in `portal/DrossTeleporter.java`:
  - `toDross` should target the hub.
  - Remove `buildReturnPortal`.
  - `toOverworld` should always go to `PortalSite`.

  The `dross_return_portal` data in `DrossPortalTravel` is no longer needed.
- **Orange colors to move onto `DrossColors`:**
  - `portal/client/DrossPortalParticleProvider.java`
  - `portal/DrossArrival.java` (title)
  - `villager/DrossTrader.java` (`GLOW_COLOR`)
  - `registry/ModItems.java` (spawn egg)
  - `enchant/NecromancyEvents.java` (`WISP_DUST`)
  - `enchant/RisenUndead.java` (`ORANGE_DUST`, `GLOW_COLOR`)
  - The texture PNGs.
- **Compass:** `villager/DrossCompass.target` currently uses `offset(1,1,0)`, which assumes an X-axis frame. Switch it to `PortalSite.getOpeningCenter`.
- **Trader:** `DrossTrader.mobInteract` is where hand-ins and dialogue hook in. `buildOffers` and `addMissingOffers` are replaced by the new shop plus a one-time migration that clears old offers.
- **Saved data:** copy the `villager/TraderSpawnData` pattern (`computeIfAbsent` on the Overworld's data storage).
- **Readable items:** vanilla only opens the book screen for `Items.WRITTEN_BOOK`. The Weathered Letter and the Guide Book open `BookViewScreen` from Quest's client code.
- **Castle file:** a structure block saves to `run/saves/<world>/generated/dross/structures/portal_castle.nbt`. Copy it to `src/main/resources/data/dross/structures/`.
- **Old test worlds** keep their bare frame and old portals. Test the castle and hub in a **new world**.

## Verify over time
Built and passing the headless check, but not yet tested in-game. Check these during future play tests and remove each line once confirmed.
- **The Rat King (1C) and his showcase:** in a superflat world (cheats on) `/dross showcase boss_designs ratking` spawns Gnawrick 1C (broad shoulders) with a sign; he matches `boss-previews/blightwood/rat_king/ratking_1c_gnawrick_*`; his fist grips the staff across its knuckles and the staff's head is all green; every chat button: Sleep (curled up, the staff lying in front of him, never through him or the floor; Zzz and snores), Wake up (eep!, a yawn, he picks up his staff with a tap), Stand (his ears never pass through the staff), Walk (on tiptoe), Burrow (he digs a spot about a block ahead, then dives in head first: the mound is where his head went in), Pop up (a rumble, out of that mound with a pop, the staff straight up out of the hole), Rage! (his left fist goes up out to his side and pumps with every stomp, never through his head; steam puffs with the choo-choo-chooo whistle; a screech; he lifts the staff and smashes its foot into the ground in front of him with dust and green sparks), Die (squeal, thud and boing, clatter, clink; the X's only on the front of his face); the Rat King spawn egg (Dross tab) makes one that wanders about, walking without sliding feet, and snickers.
- **Rat showcase and eggs:** `/dross showcase rats` puts the Plague Rat (R3) and the Plague Ravager (E3, bigger, glowing red eyes) on pedestals with signs; each matches `boss-previews/blightwood/rat_king/rats/`; their green spots and boils are the same on both sides; Idle: the rat sniffs, rears up and grooms, the Ravager hisses, bristles, lashes its tail and lunges; Run: they gallop on the spot; right-click switches one; the boils and the red eyes glow at night. The Plague Rat and Plague Ravager spawn eggs spawn that rat, which wanders about, running as it moves, and squeaks or growls.
- **Rat King sounds:** `/dross showcase boss_sounds ratking` lists every sound with a [play] button; each plays (the same as `boss-previews/blightwood/rat_king/sounds/index.html`); none sounds like Minecraft's; the showcase kings make their animations' sounds at the right moments and no idle chatter.
- **Chapel next to the nearest usable village:** in a new world, the log lists the villages checked and a "picked the village at …" line; `/dross goto trader` shows the chapel right beside that village's houses (never touching them) with a 3-wide path in the village's path material to the nearest street (smooth sandstone in a desert village).
- **Bump levelling:** on bumpy ground, bumps under the chapel are cut (grass put back on top) and dips filled, up to 3 blocks each way; the doorstep sits within 1 block of the floor; nothing floating, no walls of dirt inside.
- **5 villages at once:** the log shows "batch 1 … generating 5 village(s) at once" and one result line per village, nearest first; no lag spikes ("Can't keep up") while they generate.
- **Expanded search to 5,000 blocks:** in a world where the near villages are cramped, it moves on to the next batch and wider squares and still finds a village (the world that once sent him ~700 blocks to the plains is a good test).
- **Emergency chapel near spawn:** with Generate Structures OFF, the log shows the EMERGENCY warning and the chapel stands on flat ground near spawn, door facing spawn.
- **Letter's location hint:** the letter's direction and X/Z match where he actually lives (the picked village, or "in the wilds" for the emergency spot); a letter looted before he spawned rewrites itself with the real location once he has.
- **The Fallen Cathedral is the portal site:** in a new world (cheats on) `/dross goto cathedral` puts you on its altar steps in front of the frame; the Rift Key lights a 3x5 portal; rejoining doesn't build a second cathedral; the compass points at its frame.
- **New site placement:** in a few new worlds, the log says where the site went ("Dross portal site" lines); it's 3,000–10,000 blocks from spawn in an allowed biome (plains, desert, taiga, forest or snowy), with no water or village nearby; the ground under it was nearly flat to begin with and the edges step down at most 1 block at a time (no cliffs or dirt walls); only trees touching it were removed, each one whole (no floating leaves or half trees), and the rest of the forest is untouched; the compass, `/dross goto cathedral` and the hub's exit portal all lead there; the dead plants and cracked blocks of the leak are around it.
- **The Shattered Spire is the Dross hub:** going through the cathedral portal lands you inside the spire's hall on the top step in front of the lit blue exit portal, facing the gate, with the arrival title and notes; `/dross goto hub` lands on the same step; the exit portal takes you back to the cathedral's frame; the steps up the front of the spire are walkable.
- **The quest trader wears The Archivist:** `/dross goto trader` shows him in skin 2 (bald scholar, glowing spectacles, blue book); a spawn-egg trader wears it too.
- **Golem showcase (3G Saddleback):** `/dross showcase boss_designs colossus` spawns 3G (dormant) with its sign; its craters glow with lava while dormant; it has 3H's horns and big back spikes; no volcano or spike hangs off or sticks through the body from any side; the walk's arms move smoothly (no snapping, no seam at the loop); the chat buttons replay the eruption (it braces and leans in, the blasts shoot out along the volcanoes, then lava pours down the shell), the shell breaking (the lava volcano growing out), the lava core (its volcanoes erupting along their own angles all the time, with particles), the death (steam, then an obsidian statue that stays one) and the walk; right-click and sneak + right-click work on one golem; the golems survive a rejoin and Peaceful; nothing flickers (z-fighting).
- **Renamed commands:** tab-completing `/dross ` offers `goto`, `trader`, `quest`, `showcase` and `boss`; `/dross goto ` offers `cathedral`, `hub` and `trader`, and each works; `/dross trader sendhome` sends him home; `/dross showcase ` offers `boss_designs`, `rats`, `boss_sounds` and `trophies`; the golem showcase's chat buttons (`/dross showcase boss_designs colossus <control>`) and the arena's summon button work; the old names (`/dross site`, `/dross hub`, `/dross trader home`, `/dross showcase golems`...) are unknown commands.
- **Colossus trophies:** `/dross showcase trophies colossus` places the five designs in a row in front of you, each on a small blackstone pedestal facing you with a sign ("No. N", its name, its look); running it again replaces the row; each matching `boss-previews/cinder_wastes/cinder_colossus/trophies.png` (pose and look), fitting its block, centred, with no base; the lava cracks glow softly, bright bands of lava keep sliding down the streams (no flicker, no jump when a band loops), lava drips off now and then and the craters smoke; the creative tab shows the five as 3D figures with their names in the tooltip; one placed from the tab faces you, needs a block under it, and breaking it (any tool or by hand) drops it with the same design; right-clicking a placed one turns it to the next pose (1 > 2 > 3 > 4 > 5 > 1) at once (figure, glow, lava streams, light), still facing the same way, with a quiet click and a little ember puff; holding a block, right-click changes the pose and sneak + right-click places the block; broken after a change, it drops as the design it showed; the tooltip has the gray hint "Right-click to change its pose"; in adventure mode it can't be changed; in the hand and on the ground it's the figure. Beat a Colossus (with a second player nearby if possible): a design 1 trophy goes straight into each player's inventory with a pickup pop; with a full inventory it drops 1 block in front of you instead (at your feet if you face a wall), the other player can't pick up yours, it survives lava, fire and explosions and never despawns; `/kill` on a Colossus gives none.
- **Weak ranged hits and faster minions:** shooting the Colossus with a bow in each stage takes less and less off its bar (about half a sword hit's worth in the Shell, a third in Cracking, a sliver in the Lava Core), with a stone clink and sparks where the arrow hits in the Shell and Cracking stages and a hiss and a puff of smoke in the Lava Core; sword hits do as much as before; the stone and lava golems keep up a bit better, and their walk keeps pace (feet don't slide).
- **Minion spawn eggs:** the Stone Golem and Lava Golem spawn eggs in the Dross tab spawn a wild one: it doesn't crumble, despawns when you walk away, and the lava golem only hisses and blows up when you're within 2.5 blocks.
- **Terrain v1, the land (new world):** rolling forest round the hub, cliffs and knolls in rocky areas, Cinder mesas, the Gravecrags' snowy peaks far from the hub; no caves; the five biomes look like their descriptions (step 1, `TERRAIN_REPORT.md`); `/dross goto biome <name>` lands on safe ground in each.
- **No Nether Scar** (removed October 17): in a new world `/dross goto biome ` offers five biomes (no `nether_scar`), and no netherrack, nylium, crimson or warped fungi or Scar blocks turn up in new land; the four Scar blocks are still in the creative tab and can be placed by hand (Scar Roots on netherrack or nylium, a Soulwick on soul soil, glowing).
- **Terrain v1, sky and fog:** blue night fog in each biome's colour, the blue glow over the sky, thick green fog in Blightwood, no clouds; nothing flickers.
- **Terrain v1, blocks:** every terrain block and material in the creative tab looks right in the world (glowing Stormvine, Wispbloom, Stormite crystals, Gloomcaps, Storm Lichen, Soulwick; calm Scarred Netherrack cracks; poison-green Blightwood); axes strip silverwood; a sapling grows with bone meal (four in a square grow a great silverwood); gold ore drops nuggets, Stormite needs an iron pickaxe.
- **Terrain v1, the hub and survivors:** the spire sits level in a forest clearing (no cliffs, no dirt walls, no half trees); arrival on the step as before; the Blind Oracle and the Ragged Seeker stand on the dais, walk together round the spire, talk when right-clicked (the Seeker calls the Oracle "he"), pop back at half health, and hostile mobs ignore them.
- **Terrain v1, Cinder Wastes mobs:** only stone golems spawn there, with lava golems about 1 in 5, always near lava; no zombies or skeletons; the other biomes spawn as before.
- **Terrain v1, the volcano lair and the fight:** `/dross goto lair colossus`: the volcano (smoke, lava streams and pools, the cave mouth), the cavern (obsidian and magma floor, glowing fissures, stalactites); the walk in from the land to the cave mouth never needs a jump over 1 block (on steep land a ramp leads down to it); stepping onto the floor makes the Colossus rise; the first hit and both stage changes roar and throw you ~15 blocks; 4 golems at Cracking, 4 lava blobs at the Lava Core; it charges from far away to where you stood; no rock throws in the Lava Core; it summons against your risen undead; once beaten it never returns. Several lairs: `terrain-previews/boss_sites.png` shows one per group of 3 Cinder Wastes patches; each is built (in the background, no freeze) when you first come within about 384 blocks, and `/dross goto lair colossus` says it's building it, then lands you at the nearest one's entrance.
- **Creative tab order:** the Dross tab reads blocks, quest items, trophies, spawn eggs, weapons and tools, then the books.
- **New Deathforged gear scale:** with a sword from the creative Dross tab books (Necromancy V plus a Deathforged book, at an anvil), risen zombies wear and hold: I leather / no sword, II leather / wooden, III gold / gold, IV chainmail / stone, V iron / stone, VI iron / iron, VII diamond / iron, VIII diamond / diamond, IX and X netherite / netherite; skeletons hold a plain bow; nothing has an enchantment shimmer; X undead are faster and hit harder, with no swirl particles and no glowing outline.
- **A regular sewer (new world, cheats on):** `/dross goto sewer` lands you at the foot of a green copper pipe's steps; it has 3 pipes; jumping in drops you into a deep pool of murky green water with no fall damage; the ladder takes you back out; the tunnels below are a real maze (branches, loops, dead ends, rooms, rough caves), dark but for a little glow lichen, with green water in the channels, thick green fog and drips; Sewer Sludge patches slow you (less than soul sand); only rats spawn down there, more Ravagers than Plague Rats; on Blightwood's surface about a third of the mobs are rats, mostly Plague Rats. Looks match `terrain-previews/sewers/`.
- **The Rat King's sewer and cave:** `/dross goto lair ratking` lands you in his cave: the domed cave lit by green candles round the nest and on the gold, glow lichen on the walls, pipes pouring into drains (the floor never floods), sludge patches, the carpet nest and its gold heaps (about 70% Dross gold ore, 30% gold blocks), and the Rat King asleep on the nest; he wakes as you come near and fights as before; the sludge slows you in the fight; beaten, he never comes back; Ravagers that spawn in the cave take more hits and bite harder. Find the royal road (banners, green candles, bones, gold; no carpet) back to the grand entrance: one huge crowned pipe (bright copper points tipped with glowing green Blight Vents, copper bands, no gold blocks, tattered red banners), green glow and spores from the Blight Vents (best at night), poisoned ground round it (bones, rat nests, sludge, cobwebs, gnawed fences), no mushrooms over it. `terrain-previews/boss_sites.png` shows how the planner picks the patches.
- **Rat King's spear charge and move table:** `/dross showcase boss_designs ratking charge` plays it on the spot (crouch with the staff levelled, a fast lance dash, the skid); in a fight he's quicker (about 4.7 blocks a second, 6.2 in the Rage), sweeps more often, throws poison much less (about a quarter to a third of the time at range), and from 5-24 blocks often charges: a 0.4 s crouch, then a dash to where you were standing (dodge sideways), 6 damage then straight into a sweep, a skid and a half-second pause if he misses; the charge's squeal.
- **Rage Wand teams:** right-click in the air: the crystal changes colour (blue, red, green, yellow, purple) and the team name shows above the hotbar; hit mobs with different colours: same-colour mobs never fight each other, not even natural enemies (a blue iron golem and a blue zombie, a blue pillager and a blue iron golem: no chasing, no swings), also when the second one is coloured a few seconds after they started fighting (they stop at once), and they stay calm after an enemy team's mob has come and gone; different colours fight until one team is left, and the winners stay raged on their team; re-hitting a mob with another colour switches it; a raged boss's golems or rats join its team; coloured dust over each raged mob, no outline.
- **Rage Wand and the warden** (fixed October 18 from the user's report): rage a warden and the Rat King on different colours: the warden goes for him, and after each of his burrows, when he's out of the ground again, it goes for him again (it may roar first) and hits him; it never just stands there.
- **Rat King tuning after the play test:** each first-phase burrow brings 10 Plague Rats (the Rage's still 7 Ravagers), and rats keep coming however many are alive; his staff sweep reaches about a block further (4.5 blocks), hits 1 heart harder (9, 11 in the Rage) and knocks you back noticeably further.
- **Boss tuning of October 17:** the Colossus erupts at 70% health (its bar a little under three quarters full); in the Shell a swipe takes about 6.5 hearts, a charge 6, a slam 3.5 (no armour, Normal); the slam throws you clearly further than the roar (about 20 blocks); the eruption's roar takes a heart, the shell burst's two; a Lava Core lob drops 4 lava pools round you (Cracking still 2). The Rat King burrows at 175, 150 and 125 (10, 12, 14 Plague Rats), rages at 100 (half his bar), then burrows at 75, 50 and 25 (8, 9, 10 Ravagers) and no more; a Ravager's bite poisons a little longer. In Blightwood (at night, any world) only rats and the odd witch spawn: no zombies, skeletons or endermen.
- **Allies leave the risen undead alone:** with Necromancy near a village (or next to an iron golem you built), your risen undead and the golems ignore each other: the golems never walk at, hit or throw your undead, and still kill real zombies, even ones standing behind your undead; a snow golem's snowballs never hit them; your tamed wolf ignores your risen skeletons but still hunts real ones; hit a golem yourself and your undead attack it, but it doesn't hit them back (it may come for you); a golem angry at you gets attacked by your undead.
- **Rimefall's animals** (step 14 part 3; a new world with cheats on, so Rimefall has its lakes and herds): `/dross showcase rimefall_mobs` puts a male, a female and a calf Rimehorn, a bull, a cow and a calf moose and a Pond Skater in a row with signs; each control button plays its animation on all of them (attack: the flick, the ram, the stab; charge: the Rimehorn's quick low dash); the male has no snow on his back, the calf a big head, the cow and the calf moose no antlers; `/dross showcase mob_sounds rimefall` plays each sound, and the Rimehorn (deep chuffs and rumbles) sounds nothing like the moose (nasal calls, woody clops). Spawn eggs: hit a Rimehorn and its pack comes for you, flicking you up into the air up close and dashing at you from afar (shoot one with a bow from far off: it rushes); feed Frostbloom to a male and a female (`/summon dross:rimehorn ~ ~ ~ {Male:1b}` and `{Male:0b}`): they walk up to each other and make a calf, which never attacks you even when grown and its pack is angry; a bull's ram hurts more than a cow's; a mother with a calf goes for you when you come within about 6 blocks of her or the calf, and gives up once she's about 10 blocks from her calf; a Rimehorn or moose you've angered runs at you head on (not sideways) and gives up after about 24 blocks or half a minute; you can hit a Rimehorn on its face, snout and rump (and a moose on its head), not only its middle; a hurt calf runs off; Pond Skaters skate very fast on ice and slower off it, rear up to stab, and hitting a leg hurts them.
- **Moose coats and the Pond Skater** (October 19-20): a moose spawned in Rimefall (or in a hunter village) has frost in its fur; one from a spawn egg in a superflat Overworld world, or met in the Silverwood Forest, has none; a calf has its mother's; the Pond Skater has no frost, snow or white specks on it, is about 20% smaller than before (its leg hitboxes still on its legs), and at night a lake or pond has at most 1-2 of them, never more than 3 in one area, and they turn up only now and then.
- **Rimefall's big lakes and herds** (step 14; a new world): `/dross goto lake` lands you on a lake's shore: a sheet of ice about 100 blocks across (some patches a smaller one), dark water under it, 4-6 open holes you can fall into that never freeze over, a gentle snowy shore (no cliffs, no dirt walls, no trees or boulders on the ice, no water spilling out); at night at most 1 or 2 Pond Skaters on it (lakes differ), 1 on a patch of ice 20 blocks across or more, none on smaller ice; 2-3 herds of 6-8 Rimehorns in each Rimefall area (1-2 in a small one), away from the lake (`/dross goto biome rimefall` and look round); lone moose (sometimes a mother and calf) in Rimefall, now and then in Silverwood Forest, never round the hub; no zombies, skeletons or bats in Rimefall.
- **The Huntsman's boss bars** (step 14): `/dross showcase bossbars huntsman` shows The Wild Hunt (design 9: a stag skull with his spear and bow crossed behind it), The Huntsman (design 11: the wolf hood with antlers) and Alpha Moose (design 1: the antler frame), draining and filling for a minute; the [angry looks] button (or `... angry`) shows the two blood-red angry looks; each drawn as in `boss-previews/rimefall/huntsman/bossbars/bars.png`, the names readable and nothing overlapping the next bar; no other designs or numbered buttons left.
- **The hunter villages, built from the templates** (step 2, October 20; a superflat world, facing open land, cheats on): `/dross showcase frostfolk_villages` builds the nine Sunken Warrens (3 to a row) and the Huntsman's two a plot a tick (no long freeze), each matching `boss-previews/rimefall/huntsman/villages/` (`index.html`); [go] stands you at each one's way in, [overview] above them all; sunken courtyards with steps down and a roasting fire, lanes between them in loops with bone arches; every igloo one of the hand-designed ones, its doorway on its courtyard's floor (or a short dug way to it), its rooms exactly as in the igloo showcase (beds, chests, the guards, lamps; every skull the right way round); the warren igloos behind the rings joined by covered passages that come into the front igloo through a gap in its wall beside open floor, lit inside; the shrine alone on the snow (villages 3, 6, 9 and the Huntsman's) with a path of lanterns to the nearest steps; 1-2 Rimeberry Patches with a path; a ring of lights round the edge; no clear ice window looking into dirt; no dirt anywhere; the pens' fences two high, the moose (regular villages) or the Rimehorn herd (the Huntsman's) staying in them and never going for you (hit one: it runs); his lodge, yard and the barn at the north, a lane and stairs up to them; run it again: a different half of the decorations, the old animals and guards gone (not doubled); at night no ice melts and no snow layer vanishes.
- **The igloos' second round and the crops** (step 14, October 20): in the showcase every skull faces into its room (the lodge's and the corral's out); the Barracks' two guards and the Long Hall's four stand in a skull, blue leather and a stone sword (the Long Hall's facing the table), and a rebuilt plot doesn't double them; the halls and the shrine have 2-wide doorways; the shrine is three domes (statue, Hall of the Fallen, Offerings Chapel); the Huntsman sleeps in a triple bed; the barn's lanterns hang on short chains from the middle of its beams; the Root House, the Lichen House and the Rimeberry Patch (No. 18-20) are full of crops at mixed ages that grow by their lanterns; the bushes are two blocks tall, right-clicking either half picks berries and it regrows; Frostroot and Snow Lichen planted on farmland, bone meal grows them; the crate and the basket craft from nine and back; Rimeberries breed Rimehorns and Snow Lichen breeds moose (a bull and a cow).
- **The igloo templates and decorations** (step 14; a superflat world, cheats on): `/dross showcase frostfolk_igloos` builds 20 igloos and 58 decorations in rows facing you, a plot a tick, each with a numbered sign; [go] stands you at each doorway; every igloo's way in a plain gap 1 wide and 2 tall (the lodge's 3 wide, 4 tall); inside, every bed whole and on its own or in a pair round a nightstand, every chest opens, nothing floats on a slab, every lamp somewhere it makes sense (no random lanterns or sea lanterns), no dark corner at night (`/time set night`), fires with a smoke vent; the halls have no beds; the lodge's antlers are inside (no holes in its wall), its skulls and the corral's face out; the barn lit at the back, a chest downstairs and one in the loft; the decorations: sleds with plank decks, the light designs, the new ideas. The user picks the igloos they want and names the decorations they don't.
- **The Huntsman's fight** (step 14, October 20; a superflat world in survival, cheats on): `/dross boss arena huntsman` builds the snowy arena, its [Summon the Huntsman] button (or the Huntsman Spawn Egg) brings him riding the Alpha Moose, calm; hit either: he blows his horn and The Wild Hunt's bar appears (one bar for both); in the saddle he thrusts his spear, shoots, whirls and throws the bola (held still 2 s, then slowed), blows the horn (pushed away, slowed), rains arrows round you with no warning (each hit frosts you), the moose charges (about 6.5 hearts) and kicks you when you're behind it, and circles you while he shoots; at 0 the moose rears, he leaps off and blows his horn, two full bars (The Huntsman, Alpha Moose); on foot he combos, leaps, throws his spear (it comes back), takes an aimed shot after a frost glint (frozen), backsteps and shoots; the moose rams, tosses you high, stomps, bellows, and stamps cracks across the snow that burst into ice spikes under you; kill one: the other's bar turns blood red and it's quicker and harder-hitting; kill both: "The Hunter Hunted" (also for a friend within 40 blocks), the bodies vanish in a puff of snow; walk more than 64 blocks away for 30 s: they're back to full and calm, him in the saddle. They never hurt each other.
- **The Huntsman's fight fixes** (step 14, October 22; a superflat world in survival, cheats on): `/dross boss arena huntsman`, summon him and walk towards him: he blows his horn when he sees you (about 30 blocks), before you hit anything; in creative he ignores you; a Pond Skater near him is hunted too; arrows and sword hits on the rider hurt him and take the Wild Hunt's bar down; the moose turns smoothly and always faces where it runs (no running sideways), its circle round you a smooth ring, its gallop and his bounce in the saddle in step with its speed, no flicker between walking and running; at the split the moose rears on the spot and he lands beside it without sliding; on foot he backs off when you come close and circles you at range while the moose comes in, and the two never walk through each other; when either dies it plays only its own death (no red flash, no tipping over, no legs through the ground); with the Rage Wand, raging either one colours both, and a raged Hunt fights a raged Rat King of another colour.
- **The cloak and the moose** (step 14, October 22): `/dross showcase boss_designs huntsman`: play every riding animation with the moose idle, walking, galloping and charging, and each of the moose's moves under him (kick, toss, stomp, bellow, eat...): his cloak never shows through the moose's back, rump, tail or hind legs; it lies on the saddle and drapes over the rump without floating far off it.
- **The Moonsilver Spear** (step 14, October 22; survival): craft it (two sticks on a diagonal, a Moonsilver Ingot at the top right); held, it's a 3D spear in first and third person, a flat icon in the inventory; a stab hurts and freezes a mob (frost, shaking, slow) for about 4 s; hold right-click and let go: it flies like a trident, pointing the way it flies, hits and freezes, and comes back to you; the Huntsman's thrown spear is the same spear, bigger; the Planted Moonsilver Spear (creative tab) stands upright about 1.75 blocks tall.
- **The mounted skulls** (step 14, October 22): from the creative tab, each skull placed on top of a fence or log stands facing you, placed against a wall hangs on it facing out (each of the four ways); the antlers and the Rimehorn's clear ice horns look right (no flicker, the ice see-through) in the hand and the inventory; kill the Alpha Moose: its skull drops; a bull moose or a Rimehorn now and then (with Looting more often), never a cow or a calf; in `/dross showcase hunter_villages` and `hunter_igloos` the antler posts, gate, lamps, trophy board, palm antlers, skull posts, skull lamps, the lodge's and barn's doors and the corrals have the 3D skulls (no birch-fence antlers left) and the spears are planted spears (no lightning rods); nothing stands inside their antlers; the barn's gables meet its end walls (no slit).
- **The October 23 fixes** (step 14): in a fight, the Huntsman in the saddle never lags, shakes or turns apart from the moose while it moves and he aims or shoots (they're one model now); at the split the moose rears, neighs, and he springs off once it's well up, lands beside it on his feet without sliding and his own model takes over; the three skulls are life size (the Alpha Moose's antlers about 5 blocks across, a bull's nearly 4, a Rimehorn's 2.8 tall), on a post, on a wall and in the trophies group; `/dross showcase hunter_igloos`: the lodge's doorway under the Alpha Moose's skull opens into a long, ribbed corridor with six skulls facing in (bulls over your head, Rimehorns lower, across from each other), lit by three lanterns, then his hall.
- **The October 23 second round** (step 14): in a fight, the moose and the Huntsman glide smoothly at a gallop (no
  hopping); hit the Alpha Moose on its rump, its back legs, its chest, its front legs and its head: every hit counts;
  his arrows from the saddle never stop on the moose's head; the skulls have no teeth (the jaw ends cleanly); the
  Cinder Colossus Spawn Egg is a dark stone egg with a glowing lava crack, the Rat King Spawn Egg his crowned rat face
  (the crown centred), the Huntsman Spawn Egg his dark slate wolf hood with two white fangs over a black face and his
  blue eyes.
- **The October 23 third round** (step 14): in the Wild Hunt (its bar 250) the Huntsman attacks while the moose
  does: up close the moose rams, tosses, stomps or bellows while he spears or blows his horn; at range it charges or
  circles while he shoots (when you're on his left), throws the bola or rains arrows; in the rain his 12 arrows shoot
  up fast out of his bow and vanish high up while still climbing, then 48 streak down out of the sky round you about a
  second later, steering after you as they fall (you take a few); against a Pond Skater they hit it too; on foot at range he never just circles: quick shots between his big
  moves; his backstep shot glides him back with the leap and he's ready as he lands (no jump forward at the end); he's
  a tenth smaller (about 4 blocks to his hood), still sitting on the saddle, his hitbox matching; the skulls are bone
  copies of the live heads (the Alpha Moose's as big as its head).
- **The fight fixes and the skulls of October 23** (step 14): in the Wild Hunt his arrows hit you even while you sprint across his line, and every arrow of his frosts and slows you; he shoots ahead past the moose's head, to his right, to his left and back over his shoulder, so the moose needn't turn side-on; right next to the moose he mostly spears you; run away and the bola comes; the moose's stamp ends in a clump of small see-through ice spikes, then a big one 1.5 blocks tall bursts up under you, throwing you up, slowing and frosting you; he goes for grown Rimehorns, moose and Pond Skaters, never calves or penned animals; at his village he's already riding his patrol round it when you arrive; skulls on posts and blocks lie level on their jaw, and their hitbox fits the skull.
- **The Huntsman's trophies** (step 14, October 23): `/dross showcase trophies huntsman` places the five in a row facing you (Riding Down, Rain of Arrows, The Rearing, The Rearing Shot, The Parting Shot), each matching `boss-previews/rimefall/huntsman/trophies/trophies.png` (its first view is what you see standing in front of it), fitting its block with nothing poking out; the ice see-through, their eyes glowing at night, the moose's breath now and then, snowflakes off No. 5's spikes; right-click turns one through the five; in the creative tab and in the hand they're the 3D figures; beat the pair (a friend within 40 blocks too): each gets a No. 1 straight into their inventory.
- **The villages on the land** (step 14, October 23; a new world, cheats on): `/dross locate villages` and [go]: each
  courtyard and its igloos sit on their own ground, the land between the parts left as it was (its trees and hills),
  the lanes climbing between courtyards at different heights with a stair at each step (and the passages between
  them too), the decorations each standing on solid ground (nothing floating, nothing buried), no flattened ring round
  the village; no packed-ice squares in the floors; the barn's corral posts topped with skeleton skulls; no moose or
  Pond Skaters turning up inside the village (only the penned ones); a penned moose or Rimehorn that gets out of its
  pen fights back like a wild one.
- **Hunter villages in Rimefall** (step 14, October 23; a new world, cheats on): `/dross locate villages` lists the 5 nearest with [go] buttons (the Huntsman's in amber); each [go] (and `/dross goto village`) lands at a village's way in, on levelled snowy ground that steps gently back to the land (no cliffs or dirt walls), no trees, boulders or ice spikes in it, never on or by a big lake; `/dross goto lair huntsman`: within a few seconds he and his Alpha Moose come, him in the saddle, calm, already riding his patrol round the village (a loop just outside its lights); walk up in survival: he spots you and the fight starts; beat both: going back, he never comes again; on Peaceful he never comes.
- **The Frostfolk** (step 14, October 24; a superflat world, cheats on): `/dross showcase frostfolk` lines up eleven
  of them about 2 blocks tall (the warriors in front, the workers and the elder behind) on snow with signs, matching
  `boss-previews/rimefall/frostfolk/frostfolk.png`: a pillager's build, blue hoods each in its own style (the farmer's
  with a Rimehorn's horns), no face under the hood but black with two blue eyes that glow at night, gloves (no skin
  anywhere), the elder's small white scar over his right eye (no eye there: one blue eye) and his white beard; each
  holds its tool in its fist, never through its arm: the spears, the pike, the crook, the hoe and the rod upright out
  of the front of the fist, the axe and the cleaver held out ahead like a Minecraft tool; the archer's bow on his back
  until he shoots, the elder's sword in its sheath on his back until he strikes (he reaches over his shoulder for it),
  the tanner's hands empty; the fisher's fish hanging from his hook; each button plays its animation on all of
  them (the archer shoots, the horn-blower blows his horn); right-click one: a line in chat and its voice (the elder
  sounds older and has seven lines). The Dross tab has three Frostfolk eggs (Warrior, Worker, Elder): the warrior and
  worker eggs bring a different calling of their kind each time. From the eggs (survival): they wander and hum; hit one
  and it fights back until you walk about 16 blocks away; kill one: it tips over and flashes red like a vanilla mob,
  every horn-blower blows his horn and the others rush you with a shout until you leave their village; hit the elder, walk well away and come back after 10
  minutes: he's calm; a Pond Skater spawned beside them fights them, and when one hurts a villager the others near come
  to help.
- **The villages' frame rate** (step 14, October 24): in the Huntsman's village it stays smooth (measured about 180-280
  fps on this computer, from 47-84 before); 64 and 65 villagers there, a regular village 15-35, never more than 2 in a
  Barracks (the other warriors in the courtyards); their fires look and crackle like campfires with only a wisp of
  smoke now and then; Rimehorn herds and the Huntsman look the same as before (their ice and glow intact).
- **The Rage Wand on the Huntsman** (step 14, October 24): rage the Huntsman (or his moose) one colour and the Rat King
  or the Cinder Colossus another: the Wild Hunt fights them with its real moves (spear, arrows, the rain, the bola,
  the horn; the moose's charge, kicks, bellow and stamp), never just headbutting, and when the Colossus's golems climb
  out and come for them they turn on the golems first, then go back to the Colossus; the archer Frostfolk carries his
  bow level through the middle of his fist (raising it upright to shoot), a brown box of a quiver on his back with
  four small ice fletchings sitting in its top, lined up with it (the elder's sheath one straight line too); Pond Skaters go for the Huntsman, the Alpha Moose, moose and Rimehorns, and those
  fight back.
- **The Huntsman's fourth round of October 25**: in the bedrock arena, rage him and the Colossus on different colours:
  after the split, a golem that goes for him gets his spear, and his arrows and rain at a golem never hit the Colossus
  behind it; his arrow rain lands close round you (you take several, half a heart each, and they don't shove you);
  backed against the arena's wall he slides along it instead of running into it, and the moose circles back the other
  way at a wall; in any village no igloo cluster has more than 3 passages. (The Alpha skulls, the spear's
  enchantments and the moose mother: confirmed by the user, October 26.)
- **No spear through a roof** (October 26): in a new world's Frostfolk villages (`/dross locate villages` and [go]), and
  in `/dross showcase frostfolk_igloos`, no planted spear pokes up through an igloo's snow (the Twin Hunters' and the
  Workshop's now stand a step in from the wall).
- **The bedrock test arena** (October 25): `/dross boss arena bedrock` (cheats on) builds a square bedrock floor
  61 blocks across round you with glowstone set in it, bedrock walls 8 high with glowstone bands, open sky; its three
  buttons summon the Cinder Colossus, the Rat King and the Huntsman; at night (or in the Dross) nothing spawns in it
  by itself, and the bosses' fights work in it (the Colossus's charge stops at the wall, the Rat King burrows in the
  bedrock floor, the Huntsman's rain falls in it).
- **The way in's moose skulls** (step 14, October 24): every Frostfolk village (`/dross locate villages` and [go]) has
  a moose skull with its antlers on a tall post either side of the top of its main steps, facing you as you come in.
- **Villagers in the villages** (step 14, October 24; a new world, cheats on): `/dross locate villages` and [go]: the
  village is busy (a regular one 15-35 villagers, the Huntsman's 64-65): the elder in the meeting hall, the butcher in
  the Cookhouse, a farmer in each farm, warriors in the Barracks, people in their homes and round the courtyards' fires;
  none stuck in a wall, a bed or a doorway; they wander but stay round their part of the village; hit one and it comes
  after you, then walks back; the Huntsman fighting near them never hurts them; `/dross showcase frostfolk_villages` built
  twice doesn't double them. Watch the frame rate in the Huntsman's village (about 65 of them).
- **The Huntsman's chase** (step 14, October 24; survival, at his village): let him spot you and run straight away
  from the village: he chases you, then about 60 blocks past his patrol loop stops and keeps shooting (arrow rain too)
  while you're within about 40 blocks; get further (or out of Rimefall): they let you go and walk back home, still hurt
  (they never vanish); come back within 3 minutes: they're still hurt and fight on; stay over 64 blocks away for 3
  minutes: both back at full health (a dead one back too), him in the saddle on his patrol; stand just outside
  Rimefall near his loop: he doesn't start a fight with you; after the split the moose bellows less often.
- **The villages' paths and banks** (step 14, October 24; a new world, `/dross locate villages` and [go]): every path
  over the snow (to the shrine, the Rimeberry Patches) has a stair wherever it steps up a block onto ground that goes
  on, along it and where it meets the steps out of a courtyard, and no stairs round a single block; no windbreak or
  other decoration floats; no path block floats over the ground or is buried in it; round each courtyard the
  ground stays level for 2 blocks before it eases back to the land, and no bank anywhere in a village shows bare dirt
  (snow or Hoarfrost instead).
- **The Rearing Shot** (step 14, October 24): `/dross showcase trophies huntsman`: No. 4 shows the moose rearing, its
  front hooves up and its neck stretched out, him turned in the saddle with his bow drawn and the arrow level, pointing
  back over its rump (as in `boss-previews/rimefall/huntsman/trophies/trophy_4_rearing_shot.png`).
- **The Huntsman's sounds** (step 14, October 23): in a fight he howls when he spots you, then blows his goat horn; his arrows twang deep, the rain whistles down round you, a frost arrow hit crackles, the bola whirls, whooshes and wraps round you as it hits; his grunts, hurt and death sound like a man; the Alpha Moose clops when it walks and thunders at a gallop, bellows, rears with a scream, kicks, rams, stomps, its stamp cracks the ice and the spikes burst up glassy; neither chatters while frozen in the showcase. `/dross showcase boss_sounds huntsman` plays every version.
- **Lights inside the villages and their stairs** (step 14, October 22-23): `/dross showcase hunter_villages`: tall lamp poles round every courtyard and along every lane (their arms over it), courtyards and lanes lit at night with no dark corners; every staircase up out of a courtyard and to the lodge ends on a flat snow landing at the ground with a lantern post beside it, nothing in the way over it; no bone arch over the stairs.
- **The Huntsman's model fixes** (step 14, October 20): `/dross showcase boss_designs huntsman`: his spear a plain pole with a collar and its ice spike; the quiver out at his side with an ice-blue top, clear of the cloak; the cloak shaped and never through the moose at a gallop; in every shot the string never touches his hood or antlers, his bow arm straight in the rain and nearly so in the aimed shot and the backstep, his drawing arm never through his chest or hood; the bola visible, whirled above his right shoulder and thrown (`bola_throw` on foot, `ride_bola` riding; no snare throw); the leap strike's spear over his shoulder, not through his head; his death's arms in front of him, never through his chest.
- **The Huntsman's and the Alpha Moose's animations** (step 14): `/dross showcase boss_designs huntsman`: him in the Alpha Moose's saddle, on foot, and the moose alone, matching `boss-previews/rimefall/mobs/` (`index.html`); riding: `ride_thrust` (arm back, then under the antlers and back out to the right: never through them), `ride_shoot` and `ride_rain` (the bow arm straight, arrows taken from the saddle's quiver, three at a time in the rain, real harmless arrows fly), `ride_horn` (held at his lips for the whole call), and with each of the moose's moves (`back_kick`, `antler_toss`, `stomp`, `eat`...) he moves with the saddle, never floating; his cloak always lies on the rump, no quiver poking through it; `rear` or `death` on the moose carrying him: he leaps off as it rears and lands on his feet beside it without sliding ([reset] puts him back); on foot: `aimed_shot`, `arrow_rain`, `backstep_shoot` (a leap back with a shot at its top, the landing three blocks back, the spear flying back to his hand), `horn_blow` (a 2.5 s goat horn placeholder, the horn at his lips as long as it sounds), `death` (the spear slips at once and topples clear in front of him, he falls on his back and stays down); the Alpha Moose's `death` (a loud bellow as it rears, down onto its side, resting on the snow, not floating; it stays down); his hood's pelt never cuts into his mantle when he turns his head; his eyes, the moose's eyes and the ice glow at night; nothing flickers.
- **Rimefall picks** (step 13; superflat world, cheats on; the 13 models now, the description below is round 2's: also check the Huntsman three times (6A riding, 6A1 on foot, 6A2 the Alpha Moose alone), his wide grey bearskin cloak with its worn, ragged end (over the rump and hanging behind it riding, to his boots standing; fur outside, leather inside), his legs joined to his hips (thighs over the saddle, boots in the stirrups; standing, real legs), the horn across his belly, the bow across his back over the cloak, the Rimehorns at about 4.5 tall and 8 long; and the Aurora Eel's serpent eyes (a violet-teal-green gradient) and lids, its tail ending dark purple, its fins and length, the Fisher's mouth (nothing red poking out past its lips) and yellow eyes, the Huntsman 6A at about 7.7 blocks (6H's bone pauldrons and bearskin, the wolf face, the deer antlers, the ice spear resting in its leather cup on the saddle, the bow on his back, the quiver of arrows on the saddle), the tan reins running under the moose's eyes and outside its neck to his fists, the moose's antlers in one piece, the Rimehorns' coat of straight layers (nothing cutting through anything), their black eyes, the head running smoothly from neck to a short muzzle, the small horn on top of the head and the big one off the top of the nose, no solid strip under the skirt or the soldiers' capes (from below), nothing under the chin or throat, icicles touching the skirt and 7B's flat snow, the Snow Angel's clear ice spikes, the soldiers with no stray ice, and that nothing floats anywhere): `/dross showcase boss_designs rimefall` builds the snowy gallery (calcite walkway, no lights, packed ice posts with signs), the bosses first and then the regular mobs behind their own signs; all 23 stand there, each matching its picture in `boss-previews/rimefall/`; the Reaching Hand's fingers wear the Frostbitten Log's bark and its mound looks like Hoarfrost blocks; the Fisher's cabin is real blocks (spruce, glass, door, stairs, bricks) with warm windows, hanging on its line (5A) or on the ice in its jaws (5B), and its lanterns are lit; the see-through ice shows what's behind it (the Snow Angel's wings and halo, the eel's body and its glowing spine, the shark's lake ice, the Fisher's fangs, Rimehorn's horns); at night the eyes, halo, curtains, crystals and windows glow; nothing flickers and no face is missing (the models skip the faces nobody can see); the giants don't vanish when their feet leave the screen; each [go] button stands you facing that one; right-click shows its label, name, variant and height.

## Known bugs
Minor issues found during the early-game build. None block playing. Fix them when that area is next changed, and remove the line once fixed.
- **A floating Rimefall tree on some seeds** (Dimension area, found October 18): in one fresh GameTest world the terrain check `featuresperbiome` failed with "rimefall site at -6688, 6192: 1 tree(s), fallen log(s), mushroom(s) or fungi hanging in the air, the first at -6659, 70, 6225"; the next fresh world passed (157 of 157), so it depends on the seed. Not looked into yet.
- **The rat cave's grand entrance not found on some seeds** (Dimension area, found October 20): in one fresh GameTest world `SewersGameTest.ratCaveHasNestHoardAndDrains` failed with "RatCaves.entrancesNear doesn't find the grand entrance at -590, 76, -366" (the cave's own entrance and the planner's layout of it disagree there, perhaps on its height); the next fresh world passed (181 of 181). Not looked into yet.

- **A Rimehorn herd with no Rimehorns on some seeds** (Dimension area, found October 20): in one fresh GameTest world `RimefallLakeGameTest.rimehornHerdsAreThere` waited its full 10 minutes and found 0 Rimehorns at the nearest herd spot (-344, 280; chunk -22, 17), in a tiny Rimefall patch (16,896 blocks², 58 deep): perhaps no spot near it had Rimefall ground with room for them. Other seeds passed. Not looked into yet.
- **A Rimehorn herd short of animals, again** (October 24): in one fresh full run `RimefallLakeGameTest.rimehornHerdsAreThere`
  failed with "the herd at [22, -34] has 3 Rimehorns (2 males); 6-8, the first two males, expected" (all the other
  tests passed): like the herd with none above, too little room round its spot on that seed.
- **The rat cave's grand entrance not found, again** (October 23): the same failure as above in another fresh world,
  "RatCaves.entrancesNear doesn't find the grand entrance at 44, 93, -572".
- **A Rimefall lake's shore wall with a gap on some seeds** (Dimension area, found October 23): in one fresh GameTest world `RimefallLakeGameTest.rimefallLakeIsBuilt` failed with "the Rimefall lake at 648, 81, 312 isn't right: the shore at 607, 81, 356 is Air (the water could get out)" (and 608, 80-81, 356); 204 of the other 205 tests passed. Not looked into yet.

- **A Rimefall plant that couldn't stay, on one seed** (Dimension area, found October 25): in one full fresh GameTest run
  `realchunksperbiome` failed with "rimefall site at 6560, 5952: 0 flowing or open lava fluid blocks, 1 plants that
  couldn't stay" (every other test passed); like the floating tree above, it depends on the seed. Not looked into yet.
- **A village room read dark once** (Boss area, found October 24): in one full fresh GameTest run
  `HunterVillageShowcaseGameTest.villagesStandAndNeverMelt` failed with "sunken_warren_02: the room floor at -40689, 198,
  36958 has block light 0" (230 of 231 passed); the same templates passed in four other runs, so the light engine
  hadn't caught up on that busy run even after the test's retries (like the room it once read dark beside its
  lanterns). Not looked into further. Again on October 25 (another fresh world), the same floor cell: worth a look.
- **The Wild Hunt's two timers once never overlapped** (Boss area, found October 25): in one full fresh GameTest run
  `HuntsmanFightGameTest.theyAttackAtOnce` failed with "waiting for both of them to be in a move at once (the moose
  plays idle, he plays ride_idle, stage WILD_HUNT)" after its 600 ticks; the run before, with the same Huntsman code,
  passed. Not looked into yet.
- **The Huntsman's showcase test once saw his move not end** (Boss area, found October 23): in one full fresh GameTest run `HuntsmanShowcaseGameTest.huntsmanOnShow` failed with "after his spear combo he should stand (idle), but plays spear_combo" (210 of 211 passed); run on its own three times it passed every time, so it's a timing fluke under the full run's load (perhaps his land not ticking for a moment). Not looked into yet.

**Built but not yet tested in-game** (no file exists yet to test them with):
- Placing a real castle from `portal_castle.nbt`, including a castle frame that runs along Z (compass aim, `/dross goto cathedral` and arriving from the hub's exit portal).
- Placing a real hub from `dross_hub.nbt`.

### In-game test after the early-game build (new world, cheats ON)
1. `/loot give @s loot minecraft:chests/nether_bridge`: you get a Weathered Letter. Read it (rough hut location). The Dross tab appears with "A Weathered Letter".
2. `/dross goto trader`, then right-click him: the opening speech, then a reminder of what's still needed. The shop doesn't open.
3. Hand in 1 wither skeleton skull, 3 echo shards and 15 end stone, in any order.
   - Holding too few gets a "bring N" reply. Holding more takes exactly the amount needed.
   - You get the compass and "Proven Worthy".
   - Drop the compass and right-click him: you get a free replacement.
4. Hand in 1 trident, 1 Heart of the Sea and 8 amethyst shards. You get the Rift Key and "Keymaster". The shop now opens with Necromancy (32 emeralds + book) and Deathforged I (24 emeralds + book).
5. Follow the compass (`/dross goto cathedral`): the placeholder shrine, dead plants and cracked blocks nearby, drifting blue particles and an ambient sound.
6. Throw the key in (Q): blue portal, blue swirl, and the key is used up.
   - Throw a second key (creative tab) into the lit portal: it pops back out.
   - A netherite ingot does nothing.
   - Leave and rejoin: the portal is still lit.
7. Walk in: you arrive at the hub at Dross 0,0, with the blue title, the four notes, "Enter the Dross" and the Guide Book. A second trip gives no book.
8. Stay near the hub at night: no hostile mobs spawn within 48 blocks. Flint and steel on an obsidian frame does nothing in the Dross, and neither does the Rift Key.
9. Take the hub's exit portal: you arrive at the castle portal.
10. Necromancy: wisps, puffs and the Deathforged X glow are blue. The trader glows blue. "Rise!" is in the Dross tab.
11. `/dross quest reset` lets you replay the quest.

## Parked for later (do NOT build yet)
- Zombie guards at the portal site.
- Optional: the user's own castle (`dross:portal_castle`) or hub (`dross:dross_hub`) structure-block templates still override the Fallen Cathedral and the Shattered Spire if those files are ever added. The castle template path still uses the old, simpler ground preparation.
- A possible enemy in the Dross that wears skin 1, **Hooded Watcher** (`textures/entity/trader/skin_01_hooded_watcher.png`, villager model).
- Replace the recolored portal texture (a recolored Mojang nether portal, now blue) with an original one before publishing.
- Remove the Admin Sword (`AdminSwordItem`, its `ModItems` entry, model, texture (a recolored Mojang netherite sword), lang key and creative-tab entry). Its trade is already gone.
- Crops for the Overworld. (Rimefall's crops are built, step 14, but can't be eaten yet: the user, October 20, "don't worry about food and stuff, we will do that later".)
- From Terrain v1: a silverwood building set (stairs, slabs, fences, doors, signs, boats), potted Dross plants, uses for Stormite Shards and Moonsilver Ingots, caves under the Dross (beyond Blightwood's sewers, step 11), streams, the two future Cinder Wastes mobs, the user's **boss revive** feature (the lairs have no respawn now), announcing the lair's location, lavafalls in the lair's walls, and rebuilding old worlds' first-version lair as a volcano.
- Boss towers (including the golem floor), mini-bosses, the three elemental necromancers (Fire, Ice, Plague), the Earth General, the Storm King, the dragon mount, and boss exit portals. (The Fire Necromancer's golem, the Cinder Colossus, lives in its volcano lair in the Cinder Wastes, step 8; a tower floor for it comes later. The Plague Necromancer's Rat King has his design and fight, step 9; his lair beneath Blightwood and the Plague Necromancer's other two servants come later.)
- The elemental relics (Magma Wand, Frostbreaker Charm, Cleansing Flask), the Great Mystic Bow, and special arrows.
- Player-built portals (Stormvine (the block now exists as decoration), a craftable frame, building portals anywhere in the Overworld, returning to the portal you came from, protection for player-built frames).
- Config files for modpack makers (keep values as constants so a config can be added later).
- **Elemental soldiers in the Overworld** (the user, October 18, 2026: "we will go back to this soldier idea later"): soldiers of each of the 5 elements stand in that element's Overworld biomes, guarding a broken-down portal, 2 soldiers a portal (the Thawed Soldiers 9C and 9D are the first two, ice). The user's biomes: **Fire** (7): Desert; Savanna, Savanna Plateau, Windswept Savanna; Badlands, Eroded Badlands, Wooded Badlands. **Ice** (10): Snowy Plains, Ice Spikes; Snowy Taiga, Taiga, Old Growth Pine Taiga, Old Growth Spruce Taiga; Frozen River, Snowy Beach, Frozen Ocean, Deep Frozen Ocean. **Poison** (11): Swamp, Mangrove Swamp; Mushroom Fields; Dark Forest; Jungle, Sparse Jungle, Bamboo Jungle; Forest, Birch Forest, Old Growth Birch Forest, Flower Forest. **Earth** (12): Plains, Sunflower Plains; Meadow, Cherry Grove; Windswept Hills, Windswept Gravelly Hills, Windswept Forest; Grove, Snowy Slopes, Frozen Peaks, Jagged Peaks, Stony Peaks. **Thunder** (10): Ocean, Deep Ocean; Warm Ocean, Lukewarm Ocean, Deep Lukewarm Ocean; Cold Ocean, Deep Cold Ocean; River, Beach, Stony Shore.
- **Rimefall's roles** (the user, October 18, 2026): the Snow Angel is the Ice Necromancer and the Reaching Hand its summon; its three servants are the Aurora Eel, the Huntsman and the Fisher Below (the Eel's and the Fisher's fights later: "they'll have their own little things going on"). **The hunter village**: 4-5 igloos in Rimefall with hunters like the Huntsman but about 2 blocks tall, the Huntsman their alpha (his home, later). Rimehorn meat (it will give more saturation than hunger).
